- **medley-review**: Review and propose patches for Bob Proulx's medley and the savannah-medley-scripts deploy config (the software that configures GNU Savannah hosts). Use whenever reviewing, hardening, or patching these repos, or matching their "house idiom" in new code. Covers the concrete medley/ Savannah shell + Perl conventions to replicate, the rule that classic-Perl and terse-shell style are intentional (match, never modernize), treating in-code TODOs as maintainer reminders rather than a worklist, and a threat-model-first gate that must clear before any change is proposed as a security/privacy "improveme… ([inputloop/vibrobeast](https://github.com/inputloop/vibrobeast/tree/HEAD/plugins/vibrobeast-skills/skills/medley-review) · 4 copies)
